```
117	  /^Armor_Head_\d+\.png$/,         // 头甲可选槽(0=无头盔查询)
118	];
119	function vuiKeyMiss(name: string): void {
120	  const isFallback = VUI_FALLBACK_SAFE.some((re) => re.test(name));
121	  if (isFallback) { _vuiFallbackMisses.add(name); return; }  // 静默:F5 仍可见
122	  if (_vuiKeyMisses.has(name)) return;
123	  _vuiKeyMisses.add(name);
124	  // 调用点(首帧非本模块处)辅助定位:错误栈在此不可靠,给最近消费提示
125	  const near = _lastVuiConsumer ? ` 最近消费:最近一次 vui() 前 3 帧@${_lastVuiConsumer}` : '';
126	  console.warn(
127	    `[vui失配] '${name}' — 清单无此键。检查:①须带 .png 后缀 ②键拼写(vanilla-ui.json 为准) ` +
128	    `③若是新素材先跑 node scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs 重建清单${near}`,
129	  );
130	}
131	/** vui() 调用方上下文记录(失配时给"谁在查"线索;只留最近 3 个消费点) */
132	const _vuiConsumerRing: string[] = [];
133	let _lastVuiConsumer = '';
134	export function noteVuiConsumer(where: string): void {
135	  _lastVuiConsumer = where;
136	  _vuiConsumerRing.push(where);
137	  if (_vuiConsumerRing.length > 3) _vuiConsumerRing.shift();
138	}
139	
140	/** 整图硬 alpha：alpha ≥128 → 255，<128 → 0（并清零 RGB），消除提取 PNG 的半透明镶边 */
141	function hardAlpha(img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement): HTMLCanvasElement {
142	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
143	  c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
144	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
145	  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
146	  const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
147	  const px = d.data;
148	  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) {
149	    if (px[i + 3] >= 128) px[i + 3] = 255;
150	    else {
151	      px[i] = 0; px[i + 1] = 0; px[i + 2] = 0; px[i + 3] = 0;
152	    }
153	  }
154	  ctx.putImageData(d, 0, 0);
155	  return c;
156	}
157	
158	export class SpriteAtlas {
159	  data = atlasJson as unknown as AtlasData;
160	  resources = resourcesJson as unknown as ResourcesData;
161	  vanilla = vanillaJson as unknown as VanillaData;
162	  images = new Map<string, ImageBitmap | ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement>();
163	  /** ★ImageBitmap 化(2026-08-14 根治):drawImage(HTMLImageElement) 走浏览器
164	   *  懒解码缓存,GPU 压力下被驱逐→静默重解码(三份 trace 14-21 万次解码风暴)。
165	   *  createImageBitmap = 自持已解码像素(原版 Texture2D 语义):绘制永不重解码,
166	   *  close() = Dispose。?bitmap=0 走旧 Image 路径(逃生门) */
167	  static readonly USE_BITMAP = typeof createImageBitmap === 'function'
168	    && !(typeof location !== 'undefined' && new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('bitmap') && location.search.includes('bitmap=0'));
169	  vimages = new Map<string, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement>(); // 原版 PNG（干净像素，不做 hardAlpha）
170	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/，干净像素不 hardAlpha——UI 有抗锯齿边缘） */
171	  uiimages = new Map<string, ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement>();
172	  private uiFiles = (vanillaUiJson as { files: Record<string, string> }).files;
173	  /** 人工标注（annotator.html 导出）：sheet → spriteName → 方位标签 */
174	  annotations: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = {};
175	
176	  async load(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
177	    // 封面/Splash_*:Maples 源包的 1920×1080 启动插画(每张 ~17MB 解码 + hardAlpha
178	    // canvas 拷贝),全仓无消费方(菜单用 vanilla-ui/Logo)——启动即死重,跳过
179	    const files = Object.keys(this.data.files).filter((f) => !/封面\/Splash_/.test(f));
180	    // 原版 vanilla 素材与 vanilla-ui 贴图全部不在启动预载(8550 请求/主菜单 2GB 根因):
181	    // 图块/墙/NPC 表 → Game.newWorld/loadWorld 里 preloadVanillaWorld() 预载
182	    // (onWorldReady 之前完成,首帧 chunk 烘焙无回退);物品图标 → vicon 按需
183	    // 懒加载 + 进世界后 prefetchIcons() 后台补齐;UI 贴图 → vui() 按需
184	    // 懒加载(全部 11 处消费方每帧重查,首帧 null 自兜底)
185	    const vfiles: string[] = [];
186	    const uifiles: string[] = [];
187	    let done = 0;
188	    const total = files.length + vfiles.length + uifiles.length;
189	    await Promise.all([
190	      ...files.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
191	        const img = new Image();
192	        img.onload = () => {
193	          // 根源处理：整图硬 alpha —— 抗锯齿半透明像素（提取 PNG 的灰/黑镶边来源）
194	          // 二值化为 0/255，所有消费方（tile/墙/图标/角色）统一获得干净像素
195	          this.images.set(f, hardAlpha(img));
196	          done++;
197	          onProgress?.(done / total);
198	          resolve();
199	        };
200	        img.onerror = () => resolve();
201	        img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
202	      })),
203	    ]);
204	    // 人工标注（可选，缺失时回退）
205	    try {
206	      const r = await fetch('sprites/annotations.json');
207	      if (r.ok) this.annotations = await r.json();
208	    } catch { /* 无标注 */ }
209	  }
210	
211	  // ---- 原版素材 API（无 Unity y 翻转，按 TEdit 网格寻址） ----
212	
213	  /** 原版图块元数据 */
214	  vmeta(sheetId: number): VanillaTileMeta | null {
215	    return this.vanilla.tiles[String(sheetId)] ?? null;
216	  }
217	
218	  /** 原版图块表取帧（col,row 从 0 起）。越界/缺失返回 null */
219	  vframe(sheetId: number, col: number, row: number): DrawRect | null {
220	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
221	    if (!m) return null;
222	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);  // 懒加载兜底(正常路径 preloadVanillaWorld 已就绪)
223	    if (!img) return null;
224	    if (col < 0 || row < 0 || col >= m.cols || row >= m.rows) return null;
225	    return { img, sx: col * m.stride[0], sy: row * m.stride[1], sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
226	  }
227	
228	  /** 原版表内任意像素偏移取帧（style/显式帧：18px 步长的 frameX/frameY 直用） */
229	  vframeAt(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number): DrawRect | null {
230	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
231	    if (!m) return null;
232	    // 与 vframe/vrect 同语义懒加载（ensureVImage）：place_v_* 物品图标走本方法,
233	    // 此前直接 vimages.get——表未载时不发加载请求,宝箱内家具类物品图标永久回退
234	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
235	    if (!img) return null;
236	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + m.grid[0] > img.width || fy + m.grid[1] > img.height) return null;
237	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
238	  }
239	
240	  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
241	  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
242	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
243	    if (!m) return null;
244	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
245	    if (!img) return null;
246	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
247	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };
248	  }
249	
250	  /** 原版 NPC 贴图表取帧（纵向帧条，frameIdx 0-based）。
251	   *  未登记的 id 懒加载 vanilla/NPC_{id}.png（帧数来自 vanilla-npcs.json），首帧返回 null 下一帧生效 */
252	  private lazyNpcMeta = new Map<string, VanillaNpcMeta>();
253	  /** ⚠仅适用【纵向帧条】NPC 表。横向变体横条 NPC（如 594 风气球 = 8 列×32px 变体,
254	   *  Main.cs:23383 Frame(8,1,ai[2])）走此路径会把整条横排画出来——此类 NPC 必须
255	   *  在 Renderer.drawEnemy 加专属分支按列切片（见 drawWindyBalloon）。 */
256	  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
257	    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
258	    if (!m) {
259	      const key = String(npcId);
260	      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
261	      if (!m) {
262	        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
263	        const img = this.ensureVImage(sheet); // 懒加载+去重+失败负缓存(2026-08-13 前手动 new Image 无 onerror:404 时每次调用重发请求)
264	        if (!img) return null;
265	        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
266	        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
267	        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
268	        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
269	        m = meta;
270	      }
271	    }
272	    // 已注册路径同样走 ensureVImage(2026-08-13 前直取):预载失败(onerror 静默)时
273	    // NPC 永不显示——现在 miss 会触发重载,每帧活画自愈
274	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
275	    if (!img) return null;
276	    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
277	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };
278	  }
279	
280	  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
281	  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
282	  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
283	
284	  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
285	    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
286	  }
287	
288	  /** 原版杂项单图（呼吸气泡等） */
289	  /** 杂项单图(旗帜头像/链条/Boss 部件/Glow 叠画等,全为每帧活画)——
290	   *  miss 走 ensureVImage 触发懒加载:未就绪返回 null,消费方下帧自愈
291	   *  (2026-08-13 前 vimages.get 直取——脱离 VANILLA_MISC 预载即永不出现) */
292	  vmisc(path: string): DrawRect | null {
293	    const hit = this.vimages.get(path); // 命中直接返回(node 测试环境的已注入项同样有效)
294	    if (hit) return { img: hit, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: hit.width, sh: hit.height };
295	    if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null; // node 测试环境:无 Image,不触发加载
296	    const img = this.ensureVImage(path);
297	    if (!img) return null;
298	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
299	  }
300	
301	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
302	   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底)。
303	   *  ★键必须带 .png 后缀(uiFiles 键全部带)——裸键恒 null 且连请求都不发;
304	   *  每键 warn 一次(F5 报告 warn 环自动留痕;全屏地图 MapBG/Map 键失配由此类
305	   *  bug 实锤,2026-08-13) */
306	  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
307	    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
308	    if (!path) {
309	      vuiKeyMiss(name);
310	      return null;
311	    }
312	    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
313	    if (!img) return null;
314	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
315	  }
316	
317	  private _uiPending = new Set<string>();
318	  /** UI 贴图失败负缓存(与 ensureVImage._vImageFailed 对称,2026-08-13 补):
319	   *  清单内但 404 的键若不加终态标记,每帧重查的消费方会每帧重发请求 */
320	  private _uiFailed = new Set<string>();
321	  private ensureUiImage(file: string): ImageBitmap | ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null {
322	    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
323	    if (hit) return hit;
324	    if (this._uiPending.has(file) || this._uiFailed.has(file)) return null;
325	    this._uiPending.add(file);
326	    const im = new Image();
327	    im.onload = () => {
328	      // ImageBitmap 桥:入表即自持已解码像素(懒解码缓存驱逐免疫)
329	      const land = (store: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => {
330	        this.uiimages.set(file, store);
331	        this._uiPending.delete(file);
332	        this._uiFailed.delete(file);
333	      };
334	      if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) land(im);
335	      else tryBitmapUpgrade(im, file, land, () => land(im));  // 失败回退+退避重试(2026-08-18)
336	    };
337	    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); this._uiFailed.add(file); };
338	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
339	    return null;
340	  }
341	
342	  /** 原版物品图标。构建期已 shelf-pack 进少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(ix/iy/iw/ih
343	   *  子矩形,见 scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs);旧单体条目(无矩形字段)回退整图语义。
344	   *  未加载时触发后台懒加载并返回 null(下帧生效) */
345	  vicon(itemId: number): DrawRect | null {
346	    const m = this.vanilla.items[String(itemId)];
347	    if (!m) return null;
348	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.icon);
349	    if (!img) return null;
350	    if (m.ix !== undefined && m.iw !== undefined && m.ih !== undefined) {
351	      return { img, sx: m.ix, sy: m.iy ?? 0, sw: m.iw, sh: m.ih };
352	    }
353	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
354	  }
355	
356	  private _iconPending = new Set<string>();
357	  private _worldPreloaded = false;
358	  /** 懒加载完成回调(Game 注册 → ChunkCache 全量标脏:晚到的表重新烘焙
359	   *  已缓存的 chunk,否则 fallback 会永久烤进 canvas) */
360	  onVImageLoaded: ((file: string) => void) | null = null;
361	
362	  /** 预载文件清单(去重+decode)。tile/wall/NPC/misc 表与图标的统一底层 */
363	  async preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
364	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
365	    let done = 0;
366	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
367	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
368	      const img = new Image();
369	      img.onload = () => {
370	        // ImageBitmap 桥(字节+解码双就绪的原生语义,替代 img.decode());
371	        // 晚到钩子须在 bitmap 落地后触发(消费方读的是表内对象)。
372	        // settled 门:进度/resolve 只结算一次;onVImageLoaded 在"失败落 Image→
373	        // 重试成功换 bitmap"路径会发第二次(=晚到表语义,触发对应 chunk 重烘,故意的)
374	        let settled = false;
375	        const land = (store: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => {
376	          this.vimages.set(f, store);
377	          if (!settled) { settled = true; done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }
378	          this.onVImageLoaded?.(f);
379	        };
380	        if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) land(img);
381	        else tryBitmapUpgrade(img, f, land, () => land(img));
382	      };
383	      img.onerror = () => resolve();
384	      img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
385	    })));
386	  }
387	
388	  /** 按图块 sheet id + 墙 id 预载对应贴图表。
389	   *  Game 用出生点区域类型扫描调用——只载画面涉及的表(出生点半径内实测仅
390	   *  22/378 张图块表),而不是全量 ~750 张(~250MB 解码) */
391	  preloadTileSheetsFor(tileSheets: Iterable<number>, wallIds: Iterable<number>): Promise<void> {
392	    const files = new Set<string>();
393	    for (const id of tileSheets) {
394	      const m = this.vanilla.tiles[String(id)];
395	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
396	    }
397	    for (const id of wallIds) {
398	      const m = this.vanilla.walls[String(id)];
399	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
400	    }
401	    return this.preloadFiles(files);
402	  }
403	
404	  /** 预载常驻杂项(树冠/液体/瀑布/电路)+ NPC 表(小动物)——出生点必有,量小全载 */
405	  preloadMiscAndNpcs(): Promise<void> {
406	    return this.preloadFiles([
407	      ...VANILLA_MISC,
408	      ...Object.values(this.vanilla.npcs ?? {}).map((n) => n.sheet),
409	    ]);
410	  }
411	
412	  /** 预载世界渲染所需原版表(全量,~750 张)。仅调试/兜底用;正常路径走
413	   *  preloadTileSheetsFor + preloadMiscAndNpcs 的出生点子集 */
414	  async preloadVanillaWorld(): Promise<void> {
415	    if (this._worldPreloaded) return;
416	    this._worldPreloaded = true;
417	    await Promise.all([
418	      this.preloadTileSheetsFor(
419	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.tiles).map(Number),
420	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.walls).map(Number),
421	      ),
422	      this.preloadMiscAndNpcs(),
423	    ]);
424	  }
425	  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
426	   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
427	   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因)。
428	   *  烘焙追踪(bakeTracker,2026-08-13):ChunkCache 烘焙期间置 _baking,此处
429	   *  miss 发起加载时 note、onload 到达时 onLoaded——烘焙消费的任何懒取贴图
430	   *  晚到自动触发重烘焙,不再依赖 Game.ts 的前缀白名单(白名单保留作纵深) */
431	  bakeTracker: { _baking?: boolean; note(file: string): void; onLoaded(file: string): void } | null = null;
432	  ensureVImage(file: string): ImageBitmap | ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null {
433	    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
434	    if (hit) return hit;
435	    // ★note 先于 pending/failed 早退(2026-08-19 用户实报"生命树贴图不及时,
436	    // 手动破坏才渲染"):加载已在飞行中(预载/他人发起)时烘焙期 ensure 会早退,
437	    // 曾把 note 一起吞掉 → 表晚到无人重烘 = 缺表 fallback 钉死。failed 同 note:
438	    // 重试成功会二次 land → onLoaded → 重烘,链路反而闭环
439	    if (this.bakeTracker?._baking) this.bakeTracker.note(file);
440	    if (this._iconPending.has(file) || this._vImageFailed.has(file)) return null;
441	    this._iconPending.add(file);
442	    const im = new Image();
443	    im.onload = () => {
444	      // ImageBitmap 桥(2026-08-14 根治):入表即自持已解码像素;晚到/烘焙
445	      // 钩子在 bitmap 落地后触发(消费方读表内对象)
446	      const land = (store: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => {
447	        this.vimages.set(file, store);
448	        this._iconPending.delete(file);
449	        this._vImageFailed.delete(file);
450	        if (this.bakeTracker) this.bakeTracker.onLoaded(file);
451	        this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
452	      };
453	      if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) land(im);
454	      else tryBitmapUpgrade(im, file, land, () => land(im));  // 失败回退+退避重试(重试成功二次 land=晚到重烘,安全)
455	    };
456	    im.onerror = () => {
457	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
458	      this._vImageFailed.add(file);   // 永久失败:不再重复请求,供消费方分级告警
459	    };
460	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
461	    return null;
462	  }
463	  /** 表是否已确认加载失败(404 等)——与"加载中瞬态"区分 */
464	  vImageFailed(file: string): boolean { return this._vImageFailed.has(file); }
465	  private _vImageFailed = new Set<string>();
466	
467	  // ---- 资产健康只读视图(F5 调试报告 assetHealth 段;运行期防线,2026-08-13) ----
468	  failedVImages(): string[] { return [...this._vImageFailed]; }
469	  failedUiImages(): string[] { return [...this._uiFailed]; }
470	  vuiMissKeys(): string[] { return [..._vuiKeyMisses]; }
471	  /** 设计内回退 miss(静默但可审计) */
472	  vuiFallbackMisses(): string[] { return [..._vuiFallbackMisses]; }
473	  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
474	  prefetchIcons(): void {
475	    void this.preloadIcons();
476	  }
477	
478	  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
479	  /** 预载全部物品图标素材。图集化后清单 = 去重后的 ~3 张 Item_Atlas_k.png
480	   *  (此前 6059 张单体逐张请求);缓存 Promise——并发 await 的调用者
481	   *  都会等到同一批加载完成(此前旗标早退会让第二个调用者拿到假完成) */
482	  preloadIcons(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
483	    if (this._iconsPromise) return this._iconsPromise;
484	    const icons = [...new Set(Object.values(this.vanilla.items).map((m) => m.icon))];
485	    let done = 0;
486	    this._iconsPromise = Promise.all(icons.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
487	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }
488	      const im = new Image();
489	      im.onload = () => { this.vimages.set(f, im); done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); resolve(); };
490	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
491	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
492	    }))).then(() => undefined);
493	    return this._iconsPromise;
494	  }
495	
496	  /** 预载 UI 贴图按 key 前缀(如 ['Player_'] = 纸娃娃身体/发型,545 张)。
497	   *  exclude:子族前缀排除表(如 'UI_Bestiary')——面板专属子族只在面板打开时
498	   *  由 vui 懒加载自愈,不进启动/进图预载(2026-08-13 UI_ 397 键收窄用)。 */
499	  async preloadUiPrefix(prefixes: string[], onProgress?: (p: number) => void, exclude?: string[]): Promise<void> {
500	    const files = Object.entries(this.uiFiles)
501	      .filter(([k]) => prefixes.some((p) => k.startsWith(p))
502	        && !(exclude ?? []).some((e) => k.startsWith(e)))
503	      .map(([, f]) => f);
504	    return this.preloadUiFiles(files, onProgress);
505	  }
506	
507	  /** 按 UI 文件路径预载(装备中的具体 Armor 表;decode 保证首帧无解码卡顿) */
508	  async preloadUiFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
509	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
510	    let done = 0;
511	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
512	      if (this.uiimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
513	      const im = new Image();
514	      im.onload = () => {
515	        // ImageBitmap 桥(2026-08-18 imglog 实锤漏网第四站:此处曾直接 set(Image)
516	        // +decode() ——预载清单含 UI_Cursor_0(菜单起每帧画)与 Player_ 纸娃娃表
517	        // (进世界起每帧画),永久 Image = trace 残余流两大恒定家族的全部来源)
518	        // settled 门:进度/resolve 只结算一次(失败重试成功会二次 land,只换表项)
519	        let settled = false;
520	        const land = (store: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => {
521	          this.uiimages.set(f, store);
522	          if (settled) return;
523	          settled = true;
524	          done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve();
525	        };
526	        if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) { land(im); return; }
527	        tryBitmapUpgrade(im, f, land, () => land(im));
528	      };
529	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
530	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
531	    })));
532	  }
533	
534	  has(file: string): boolean {
535	    return this.images.has(file);
536	  }
537	
538	  /** 取精灵绘制矩形（Unity y 翻转已处理）。找不到返回 null。 */
539	  rect(file: string, sprite: string): DrawRect | null {
540	    const img = this.images.get(file);
541	    const entry = this.data.files[file];
542	    if (!img || !entry) return null;
543	    const s = entry.sprites.find((x) => x.name === sprite);
544	    if (!s) return null;
545	    return { img, sx: s.x, sy: img.height - s.y - s.h, sw: s.w, sh: s.h };
546	  }
547	
548	  animFrames(anim: string): DrawRect[] {
549	    const frames = this.resources.anims[anim] ?? [];
550	    return frames.map((f) => this.rect(f.file, f.sprite)).filter(Boolean) as DrawRect[];
551	  }
552	}

554	// ---- 物品图标：一律原版 Item_N（旧 Maples UI/ 图标层已整体移除） ----
555	import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
556	
557	/** DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue, 3) 的静态等价(IsFood 全表) */
558	const FOOD = { dur: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, n: 3, static: true };
559	const FOOD_IDS: number[] = [
560	  353, 357, 967, 969, 1787, 1911, 1912, 1919, 1920, 2266, 2267, 2268, 
561	  2425, 2426, 2427, 3195, 3532, 4009, 4010, 4011, 4012, 4013, 4014, 4015, 
562	  4016, 4017, 4018, 4019, 4020, 4021, 4022, 4023, 4024, 4025, 4026, 4027, 
563	  4028, 4029, 4030, 4031, 4032, 4033, 4034, 4035, 4036, 4037, 4282, 4283, 
564	  4284, 4285, 4286, 4287, 4288, 4289, 4290, 4291, 4292, 4293, 4294, 4295, 
565	  4296, 4297, 4403, 4411, 4614, 4615, 4616, 4617, 4618, 4619, 4620, 4621, 
566	  4622, 4623, 4624, 4625, 5009, 5041, 5042, 5092, 5093, 5275, 5277, 5278, 
567	  5537, 5645, 
568	];
569	
570	/**
571	 * 物品贴图动画注册表(Main.InitializeItemAnimations :3685-3722 1:1)。
572	 * 食物族:ItemID.Sets.IsFood(:258,86 项)全部为竖 3 帧条,原版注册
573	 * DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue,3) = 恒帧 0(顶部一帧);本表以 FOOD
574	 * 静态条目等价表达(切片帧高 = 总高/3)。
575	 * 物品源图是竖排多帧条(如坠星 Item_75=22×208),不切片会整条入画。
576	 * dur=tick/帧;pingPong=往返;static=true=恒帧 0(IsFood 的 int.MaxValue
577	 * 静态三帧与 NotActuallyAnimating 族)。
578	 */
579	export const ITEM_ANIMATION: Record<number, { dur: number; n: number; pingPong?: boolean; static?: boolean }> = {
580	  3581: { dur: 4, n: 4 },
581	  3580: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
582	  75: { dur: 5, n: 8, pingPong: true },    // 坠星 8 帧 PingPong 旋转
583	  575: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 547: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 520: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
584	  548: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 521: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 549: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
585	  3453: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3454: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3455: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
586	  4068: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },   // NotActuallyAnimating(:3701-3709)
587	  4069: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
588	  4070: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
589	  5644: { dur: 7, n: 9 },                 // ScryingOrb(专属着色器,垂直循环近似)
590	  // ---- 食物族(IsFood :258):竖 3 帧条,原版 int.MaxValue 恒帧 0 ----
591	  ...Object.fromEntries(FOOD_IDS.map((v) => [v, FOOD])),
592	};
593	
594	
595	/** 物品动画当前帧(DrawAnimationVertical.GetFrame 语义;tick=游戏 tick 或等价毫秒换算) */
596	export function itemAnimFrame(vid: number, tick: number): number {
597	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
598	  if (!a || a.static) return 0;
599	  const span = a.pingPong ? a.n * 2 - 2 : a.n;
600	  const idx = Math.floor(tick / a.dur) % span;
601	  return a.pingPong && idx >= a.n ? span - idx : idx;
602	}
603	
604	/** 把整条 DrawRect 按帧切片(vid 无动画原样返回) */
605	export function sliceItemAnimFrame(vid: number, ar: DrawRect, tick: number): DrawRect {
606	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
607	  if (!a || a.n <= 1) return ar;
608	  const fh = ar.sh / a.n;
609	  const f = Math.min(a.n - 1, itemAnimFrame(vid, tick));
610	  return { img: ar.img, sx: ar.sx, sy: ar.sy + Math.round(fh * f), sw: ar.sw, sh: Math.round(fh) };
611	}
612	
613	export function atlasIconForKey(atlas: SpriteAtlas, key: string): DrawRect | null {
614	  const vid = VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP[key];
615	  if (vid !== undefined) return atlas.vicon(vid);
616	  // 阶段 5:vi_<id>_* 全量物品回退原版图标
617	  if (key.startsWith('vi_')) {
618	    const id = parseInt(key.slice(3), 10);
619	    if (Number.isFinite(id)) return atlas.vicon(id);
620	  }
621	  // 阶段 5:place_v_* 放置物品回退用图块贴图首帧作图标
622	  if (key.startsWith('place_v_')) {
623	    const tk = key.slice('place_'.length);
624	    const tid = TILE_BY_KEY[tk];
625	    if (tid !== undefined) {
626	      const td = TILE_DEFS[tid];
627	      // vframeAt 而非 vframe:窄条表(如压板 135 的 16×200 竖条)cols=0 会让 vframe 判越界
628	      if (td?.vanilla) return atlas.vframeAt(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0) ?? atlas.vframe(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0);
629	    }
630	  }
631	  return null;
632	}

1011	  vi_5505_mean_goblin_s_spikes: 5505,
1012	  vi_5525_cursed_piper_flute: 5525,
1013	  // ---- 旧 UI 移除迁移补全（2026-08-09，全部 id 经 TEdit items.json 核实；
1014	  //      木镐/木斧原版不存在，用铜镐 3509/铜斧 3506 代位） ----
1015	};
1016	
1017	/** 独立加载器共用 ImageBitmap 桥(二期,2026-08-14):
1018	 *  用法:im.onload 里先照旧 set(Image),再调 upgradeToBitmap(im, b => map.set(k, b))
1019	 *  ——消费方每帧重查,下一帧起拿到的就是自持解码像素;契约零变化 */
1020	export function upgradeToBitmap(img: HTMLImageElement, onReady: (b: ImageBitmap) => void, onFail?: () => void): void {
1021	  if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) return;
1022	  tryBitmapUpgrade(img, bitmapLabel(img), onReady, () => onFail?.());
1023	}
1024	
1025	/** createImageBitmap 失败统计(2026-08-18;?imglog=1 / F5 可见)。
1026	 *  ★压力窗口期(大世界进图/GPU 预算临界)失败并不罕见——此前静默回退 Image
1027	 *  且【永不重试】= 每帧绘制的贴图(天空/群系背景)永久停在 Image 阶段,
1028	 *  解码位图被逐出时反复 LazyPixelRef(trace 残余流 ~240/s 的主源) */
1029	export const bmpFailStats = { count: 0, files: new Map<string, number>() };
1030	function bitmapLabel(img: HTMLImageElement): string {
1031	  const p = (img.src || '').split('/').filter(Boolean);
1032	  return p.slice(-2).join('/');
1033	}
1034	const BMP_RETRY_DELAYS = [10_000, 20_000, 40_000];
1035	const _bmpFailWarned = new Set<string>();
1036	function noteBmpFail(label: string, attempt: number): void {
1037	  bmpFailStats.count++;
1038	  bmpFailStats.files.set(label, (bmpFailStats.files.get(label) ?? 0) + 1);
1039	  // 每文件只警告一次(压力爆发期 50 张×3 重试=200 行会淹没警告环;计数仍全量入 stats)
1040	  if (_bmpFailWarned.has(label)) return;
1041	  _bmpFailWarned.add(label);
1042	  console.warn(`[bitmap失败] ${label} — createImageBitmap 失败(GPU 压力窗口期常见),已回退 Image`
1043	    + (attempt < BMP_RETRY_DELAYS.length ? `,${BMP_RETRY_DELAYS[attempt] / 1000}s 后自动重试` : ',放弃重试(重载页面可再试)'));
1044	}
1045	/** 带退避重试的升级:失败先让调用方落一次 Image(不缺图),10/20/40s 后重试;
1046	 *  重试成功 onReady(bitmap) 把持有方的 Image 原地换掉。
1047	 *  ★fallback 只在【首次失败】触发——后续重试失败不再重复落钩
1048	 *  (ensureVImage 的 land 会发 onVImageLoaded→chunk 重烘,重复触发=重烘风暴) */
1049	function tryBitmapUpgrade(
1050	  img: HTMLImageElement, label: string,
1051	  onReady: (b: ImageBitmap) => void, fallback: () => void, attempt = 0,
1052	): void {
1053	  let fellBack = false;
1054	  const attemptOnce = (n: number): void => {
1055	    createImageBitmap(img).then((b) => {
1056	      if (n > 0) console.log(`[bitmap重试成功] ${label}(第 ${n} 次)`);
1057	      onReady(b);
1058	    }, () => {
1059	      if (!fellBack) { fellBack = true; fallback(); }
1060	      noteBmpFail(label, n);
1061	      if (n < BMP_RETRY_DELAYS.length) {
1062	        setTimeout(() => {
1063	          if (!(img.width > 0)) return;   // 图已被释放,放弃
1064	          attemptOnce(n + 1);
1065	        }, BMP_RETRY_DELAYS[n]);
1066	      }
1067	    });
1068	  };
1069	  attemptOnce(attempt);
1070	}
1071	/** 通用 bitmap-only 加载器(2026-08-17 修 trace⑨ 收尾两个真 bug):
1072	 *  ①在飞守卫——此前未就绪期间每帧重 new Image = 请求风暴(雨/尘每粒子每帧)
1073	 *  ②失败回退存 Image——此前失败静默 = 纹理永久缺(用户报"贴图丢失")
1074	 *  ③(2026-08-18)失败回退非终态:退避重试,成功后 store(bitmap) 原地替换 */
1075	const _bmpOnlyPending = new Set<string>();
1076	export function loadBitmapOnly(
1077	  file: string, has: () => boolean, store: (x: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => void,
1078	): void {
1079	  if (has() || _bmpOnlyPending.has(file)) return;
1080	  _bmpOnlyPending.add(file);
1081	  const im = new Image();
1082	  im.onload = () => {
1083	    const land = (x: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => { store(x); _bmpOnlyPending.delete(file); };
1084	    if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) { land(im); return; }
1085	    tryBitmapUpgrade(im, file, (b) => land(b), () => land(im));
1086	  };
1087	  im.onerror = () => _bmpOnlyPending.delete(file);
1088	  im.src = `sprites/${file}`;
1089	}
1090	
1091	export const USE_BITMAP = SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP;
1092	

```

```
2	// 原版 Tiles 表无 .meta（不同于 Maples Unity 项目），按 textureGrid+frameGap 网格寻址：
3	//   帧 (col,row) 位于 (col*(gridX+gapX), row*(gridY+gapY))，帧尺寸 = textureGrid
4	// 用法：node scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs
5	import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, copyFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync, unlinkSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
6	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
7	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
8	
9	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
10	const ROOT = join(here, '..');
11	const ASSETS = join(ROOT, '..', 'terraria-assets', 'Images');
12	const TEDIT_DATA = join(ROOT, '..', 'Terraria-Map-Editor', 'src', 'TEdit.Terraria', 'Data');
13	const OUT_DIR = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla');
14	const OUT_JSON = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla.json');
15	const whitelist = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, 'vanilla-whitelist.json'), 'utf8'));

17	/** 读 PNG IHDR（8 字节签名 + 4 长度 + 4 类型后即宽高，各 4 字节大端） */
18	function pngSize(file) {
19	  const b = readFileSync(file);
20	  return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };

22	
23	const tiles = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'tiles.json'), 'utf8'));

190	const NPC_FRAME_H = {

192	  46: 40, 74: 24, 299: 32, 300: 16, 355: 16, 356: 24,
193	  359: 20, 361: 22, 362: 40, 377: 12,
194	  442: 24, 443: 40, 444: 24, 445: 22, 446: 12, 447: 16,

201	let copied = 0, missing = [];

203	for (const id of whitelist.tiles) {
204	  const t = tileById.get(id);
205	  const src = join(ASSETS, `Tiles_${id}.png`);
206	  if (!t || !existsSync(src)) { missing.push(`Tiles_${id}`); continue; }
207	  copyFileSync(src, join(OUT_DIR, `Tiles_${id}.png`)); copied++;
208	  const grid = t.textureGrid ?? [16, 16];
209	  const gap = t.frameGap ?? [2, 2];
210	  const { w, h } = pngSize(src);
211	  out.tiles[id] = {
212	    name: t.name,
213	    key: t.key,
214	    sheet: `vanilla/Tiles_${id}.png`,
215	    solid: !!t.isSolid,
216	    blend: !!t.canBlend,
217	    framed: !!t.isFramed,
218	    light: !!t.isLight,
219	    color: hex(t.color),
220	    placement: t.placement ?? null,
221	    grid, // 帧像素尺寸（蜡烛类 [16,20]）
222	    stride: [grid[0] + gap[0], grid[1] + gap[1]], // 表内帧步长
223	    frameSize: t.frameSize ?? [[1, 1]], // 每个占格数（多格家具）
224	    isStone: !!t.isStone,   // 石族（同类互连判定）
225	    isGrass: !!t.isGrass,   // 草族（strictness 2 草规则）
226	    mergeWith: t.mergeWith ?? null, // 合并目标（blend framing）
227	    cols: Math.floor(w / (grid[0] + gap[0])),
228	    rows: Math.floor(h / (grid[1] + gap[1])),
229	  };

232	// 阶段5:物品全量——shelf-pack 打包成少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(6059 张单体 → ~3 张)。
233	//  请求粒度优化:运行时 preloadIcons 只取图集,不再逐张 Item_N.png(此前 6000+ 网络请求)。
234	//  2048² 货架,按高度降序;2px gutter 防 UI 32×32 缩小采样串色;解码失败回退单体路径。
235	import { PNG } from 'pngjs';
236	const ATLAS_W = 2048, ATLAS_H = 2048, GUTTER = 2;
237	// 先 IHDR 读尺寸(不解码)→ 按高度降序排序,货架密度更高
238	const itemFiles = [];
239	for (const it of items) {
240	  const src = join(ASSETS, `Item_${it.id}.png`);
241	  if (!existsSync(src)) { missing.push(`Item_${it.id}`); continue; }
242	  try { itemFiles.push({ it, src, ...pngSize(src) }); } catch { missing.push(`Item_${it.id}(IHDR)`); }
243	}
244	itemFiles.sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
245	let atlasPage = null, atlasX = 0, atlasY = 0, shelfH = 0, atlasIdx = -1;
246	const flushAtlas = () => {
247	  if (!atlasPage) return;
248	  writeFileSync(join(OUT_DIR, `Item_Atlas_${atlasIdx}.png`), PNG.sync.write(atlasPage));
249	  atlasPage = null;
250	};
251	const newAtlasPage = () => {
252	  flushAtlas();
253	  atlasIdx++;
254	  atlasPage = new PNG({ width: ATLAS_W, height: ATLAS_H });
255	  atlasX = GUTTER; atlasY = GUTTER; shelfH = 0;
256	  console.log(`item atlas #${atlasIdx} open`);
257	};
258	for (const { it, src, w: iw0, h: ih0 } of itemFiles) {
259	  const id = it.id;
260	  let png = null;
261	  try { png = PNG.sync.read(readFileSync(src)); } catch { png = null; }
262	  const base = {
263	    name: it.name,
264	    key: it.key,
265	    createTile: it.createTile ?? null,
266	  };
267	  if (!png) {
268	    // 解码失败兜底:保持旧单体路径(同时拷贝单体)
269	    copyFileSync(src, join(OUT_DIR, `Item_${id}.png`)); copied++;
270	    out.items[id] = { ...base, icon: `vanilla/Item_${id}.png` };
271	    continue;
272	  }
273	  if (!atlasPage) newAtlasPage();
274	  const iw = png.width, ih = png.height;
275	  if (atlasX + iw + GUTTER > ATLAS_W) { atlasX = GUTTER; atlasY += shelfH + GUTTER; shelfH = 0; }
276	  if (atlasY + ih + GUTTER > ATLAS_H) newAtlasPage();
277	  PNG.bitblt(png, atlasPage, 0, 0, iw, ih, atlasX, atlasY);
278	  out.items[id] = {
279	    ...base,
280	    icon: `vanilla/Item_Atlas_${atlasIdx}.png`,
281	    ix: atlasX, iy: atlasY, iw, ih,
282	  };
283	  atlasX += iw + GUTTER;
284	  if (ih > shelfH) shelfH = ih;
285	}
286	flushAtlas();
287	console.log(`item atlas pages: ${atlasIdx + 1}`);
288	// 清理旧单体残留(图集化前的产物,已无任何引用;readdirSync+unlinkSync 避免与白名单拷贝混淆)
289	{
290	  let removed = 0;
291	  for (const f of readdirSync(OUT_DIR)) {
292	    if (/^Item_\d+\.png$/.test(f)) { unlinkSync(join(OUT_DIR, f)); removed++; }
293	  }
294	  if (removed) console.log(`removed ${removed} legacy Item monoliths`);
295	}

297	for (const id of whitelist.walls) {

300	  if (!w || !existsSync(src)) { missing.push(`Wall_${id}`); continue; }
301	  copyFileSync(src, join(OUT_DIR, `Wall_${id}.png`)); copied++;
302	  // 墙表固定 32px 帧 + 4px 间隔（TEdit WorldRenderXna texsize=32, stride=36）
303	  const grid = [32, 32], stride = [36, 36];
304	  const { w: pw, h: ph } = pngSize(src);
305	  out.walls[id] = {
306	    name: w.name, key: w.key, sheet: `vanilla/Wall_${id}.png`,
307	    color: hex(w.color), grid, stride,
308	    cols: Math.floor(pw / stride[0]), rows: Math.floor(ph / stride[1]),
309	    largeFrame: w.largeFrameType ?? 0,
310	  };
311	}

314	// NPC 贴图表（纵向帧条）：帧高手配（原版帧数存于二进制，按贴图规格配）
315	for (const id of whitelist.npcs ?? []) {

318	  copyFileSync(src, join(OUT_DIR, `NPC_${id}.png`)); copied++;

360	// ---- UI 贴图段（vui 框架专用，独立目录 vanilla-ui/ 避免与旧 sprites/UI/ 冲突） ----
361	const UI_OUT_DIR = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla-ui');
362	const UI_OUT_JSON = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla-ui.json');
363	mkdirSync(UI_OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });
364	const uiList = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, 'vanilla-ui-whitelist.json'), 'utf8'));
365	const uiOut = { files: {} };
366	const uiKeys = new Set();
367	let uiCopied = 0;
368	const uiMissing = [];
369	for (const rel of uiList) {
370	  const src = join(ASSETS, rel);
371	  if (!existsSync(src)) { uiMissing.push(rel); continue; }
372	  // 子目录下划线展平：UI/CharCreation/ColorHair.png → CharCreation_ColorHair.png
373	  const key = rel.replace(/\//g, '_');
374	  if (uiKeys.has(key)) throw new Error(`vanilla-ui 重名键: ${key}`);
375	  uiKeys.add(key);

377	  uiOut.files[key] = `vanilla-ui/${key}`;

380	// 发型表（228 张，逐张单文件）+ 帽子发型（Player_HairAlt，hatHair 头盔露出用）
381	for (let i = 1; i <= 228; i++) {
382	  const src = join(ASSETS, `Player_Hair_${i}.png`);
383	  if (!existsSync(src)) { uiMissing.push(`Player_Hair_${i}`); continue; }
384	  copyFileSync(src, join(UI_OUT_DIR, `Player_Hair_${i}.png`));
385	  uiOut.files[`Player_Hair_${i}.png`] = `vanilla-ui/Player_Hair_${i}.png`;
386	  uiCopied++;
387	  const altSrc = join(ASSETS, `Player_HairAlt_${i}.png`);
388	  if (existsSync(altSrc)) {
389	    copyFileSync(altSrc, join(UI_OUT_DIR, `Player_HairAlt_${i}.png`));
390	    uiOut.files[`Player_HairAlt_${i}.png`] = `vanilla-ui/Player_HairAlt_${i}.png`;
391	    uiCopied++;
392	  }
393	}
394	// 玩家身体帧表（skinVariant_clothStyle，40×~1120 = 20 帧×56px，灰度通道素材供纸娃娃调色）
395	for (let v = 0; v <= 11; v++) {
396	  for (let c = 0; c <= 15; c++) {
397	    const name = `Player_${v}_${c}.png`;
398	    const src = join(ASSETS, name);
399	    if (!existsSync(src)) continue;
400	    copyFileSync(src, join(UI_OUT_DIR, name));
401	    uiOut.files[name] = `vanilla-ui/${name}`;
402	    uiCopied++;
403	  }
404	}
405	// 盔甲贴图（纸娃娃装备渲染）：Armor_Head_{itemId}（20帧竖条）/ Armor_Legs_{itemId}（竖条）/
406	// Armor/Armor_{itemId}（9×4 复合网格，与玩家复合通道同构）
407	const armorFiles = readdirSync(ASSETS).filter((f) => /^Armor_(Head|Legs)_\d+\.png$/.test(f));
408	for (const f of armorFiles) {
409	  copyFileSync(join(ASSETS, f), join(UI_OUT_DIR, f));
410	  uiOut.files[f] = `vanilla-ui/${f}`;
411	  uiCopied++;
412	}
413	const armorDir = join(ASSETS, 'Armor');
414	if (existsSync(armorDir)) {
415	  for (const f of readdirSync(armorDir).filter((f) => /^Armor_\d+\.png$/.test(f))) {
416	    const key = `Armor_${f}`; // Armor/Armor_81.png → Armor_Armor_81.png（展平加前缀防撞名）
417	    copyFileSync(join(armorDir, f), join(UI_OUT_DIR, key));
418	    uiOut.files[key] = `vanilla-ui/${key}`;
419	    uiCopied++;
420	  }
421	}
422	// ---- 全量族拷贝（2026-08-13 素材差异扫描批）----
423	// 根级：除 Item_\d+（Item_Atlas 打包通道）外全拷（Gore/Glow/Extra/Cloud/ItemFlame/
424	// LiquidSlope/Chain/Mount/Rune/散件……）。子目录：UI/** → vanilla-ui 展平键；
425	// 其余（Backgrounds/Misc/TownNPCs/Accessories/Armor/SplashScreens）→ vanilla/<展平名>
426	// （父目录_文件名；重名时父目录前缀消歧）。本段幂等：重跑重复拷贝无副作用。
427	{
428	  const imgDir = join(ASSETS);
429	  const skipRoot = (f) => /^Item_\d+\.png$/.test(f);
430	  let bulk = 0;
431	  for (const f of readdirSync(imgDir)) {
432	    const full = join(imgDir, f);
433	    if (!statSync(full).isFile() || !f.endsWith('.png') || skipRoot(f)) continue;
434	    copyFileSync(full, join(OUT_DIR, f)); bulk++;
435	  }
436	  const SUBDIRS_UI = ['UI'];
437	  const SUBDIRS_MISC = ['Backgrounds', 'Misc', 'TownNPCs', 'Accessories', 'Armor', 'SplashScreens'];
438	  const flatName = (rel) => rel.replace(/\//g, '_');
439	  const usedNames = new Set(readdirSync(OUT_DIR));
440	  const copySub = (sub, toUi) => {
441	    const outDir = toUi ? UI_OUT_DIR : OUT_DIR;
442	    const seen = new Set(readdirSync(outDir));
443	    const walkD = (dir, rel) => {   // rel 含子目录前缀（UI/… → 展平为 UI_…）
444	      for (const f of readdirSync(dir)) {
445	        const p = join(dir, f);
446	        if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) { walkD(p, `${rel}${f}/`); continue; }
447	        if (!f.endsWith('.png')) continue;
448	        const key = flatName(rel + f);
449	        if (seen.has(key)) continue;   // 已有同名（含白名单产物）不覆盖
450	        copyFileSync(p, join(outDir, key));
451	        seen.add(key); bulk++;
452	        if (toUi) uiOut.files[key] = `vanilla-ui/${key}`;
453	      }
454	    };
455	    walkD(join(imgDir, sub), `${sub}/`);
456	  };
457	  for (const sub of SUBDIRS_UI) copySub(sub, true);
458	  for (const sub of SUBDIRS_MISC) copySub(sub, false);
459	  console.log(`bulk-family copied: ${bulk}`);
460	}
461	writeFileSync(UI_OUT_JSON, JSON.stringify(uiOut));
462	console.log(`vanilla-ui: copied=${uiCopied}`);
463	if (uiMissing.length) console.warn('UI 缺失:', uiMissing.join(', '));
464	
465	writeFileSync(OUT_JSON, JSON.stringify(out));
466	console.log(`vanilla-atlas: tiles=${Object.keys(out.tiles).length} items=${Object.keys(out.items).length} walls=${Object.keys(out.walls).length} copied=${copied}`);
467	if (missing.length) console.warn('缺失（白名单有但文件/数据不存在）:', missing.join(', '));
468	
469	// ---------- SW 资产预载索引(2026-08-13)----------
470	// 产出 public/assets-index.json:页面侧 AssetCache 的 P2(其余贴图)/P3(音效)枚举源。
471	// 音乐不走这里(页面从 data/Music.ts MUSIC 表枚举)。fonts/l10n 也列入 misc。
472	// 本脚本在 vanillaAtlasAuto 的 stale 源清单里 → dev 启动与 vite build 自动重跑;
473	// ★若只改了 sounds/fonts/l10n 内容,需手动 `node scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs` 或
474	//   bump AssetCache.CACHE_BUSTER 使 SW 版本缓存失效。
475	{
476	  const pub = join(ROOT, 'public');
477	  const listDir = (dir, ext) => {
478	    const d = join(pub, dir);
479	    if (!existsSync(d)) return [];
480	    return readdirSync(d).filter((f) => f.endsWith(ext)).map((f) => `${dir}/${f}`).sort();
481	  };
482	  const index = {
483	    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
484	    sounds: listDir('sounds', '.wav'),
485	    fonts: listDir('fonts', '.woff2'),
486	    l10n: ['l10n/index.json', ...readdirSync(join(pub, 'l10n')).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.json') && f !== 'index.json').map((f) => `l10n/${f}`)],
487	    miscVanilla: readdirSync(join(pub, 'sprites', 'vanilla'))
488	      .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.png') && !/^Item_Atlas_/.test(f) && !/^Tiles_\d+\.png$/.test(f) && !/^Wall_\d+\.png$/.test(f) && !/^NPC_\d+\.png$/.test(f))
489	      .map((f) => `sprites/vanilla/${f}`).sort(),
490	    miscUi: readdirSync(join(pub, 'sprites', 'vanilla-ui'))
491	      .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.png'))
492	      .map((f) => `sprites/vanilla-ui/${f}`).sort(),
493	  };
494	  writeFileSync(join(pub, 'assets-index.json'), JSON.stringify(index));
495	  console.log(`assets-index: sounds=${index.sounds.length} fonts=${index.fonts.length} l10n=${index.l10n.length} miscVanilla=${index.miscVanilla.length} miscUi=${index.miscUi.length}`);
496	}
```

```
1	// 素材管线：解析 Unity .meta 精灵切片 → 拷贝 PNG 到 public/sprites → 生成 atlas.json
2	// 用法: node scripts/build-atlas.mjs [源Assets目录] [目标目录]
3	import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, copyFileSync, readdirSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
4	import { join, relative, basename } from 'node:path';
5	
6	const SRC = process.argv[2] || '../Terraia_Maples/Assets/Sprites';
7	const DST = process.argv[3] || 'public/sprites';
8	const RES = process.argv[4] || '../Terraia_Maples/Assets/Resources';
9	
10	// ---------- .meta 解析 ----------
11	function parseMeta(text) {
12	  const guid = (text.match(/^guid: (\w+)/m) || [])[1];
13	  const sprites = [];
14	  const blocks = text.split('- serializedVersion: 2\n      name: ').slice(1);
15	  for (const b of blocks) {
16	    const name = b.split('\n')[0].trim();
17	    const rect = b.match(/rect:\n\s+serializedVersion: 2\n\s+x: (-?\d+)\n\s+y: (-?\d+)\n\s+width: (\d+)\n\s+height: (\d+)/);
18	    if (rect) {
19	      sprites.push({ name, x: +rect[1], y: +rect[2], w: +rect[3], h: +rect[4] });
20	    }
21	  }
22	  // internalIDToNameTable：fileID(213) → 切片名（RuleTile 的精灵引用靠它解析）
23	  const idToName = {};
24	  const idRe = /- first:\n      213: (-?\d+)\n    second: (.+)/g;
25	  let m;
26	  while ((m = idRe.exec(text))) idToName[m[1]] = m[2].trim();
27	  // spriteSheet 精灵块兜底：部分精灵只在这里有 name + internalID
28	  const sbRe = /^      name: (.+)$\n[\s\S]*?^      internalID: (-?\d+)$/gm;
29	  while ((m = sbRe.exec(text))) {
30	    if (!(m[2] in idToName)) idToName[m[2]] = m[1].trim();
31	  }
32	  return { guid, sprites, idToName };
33	}
34	
35	function walk(dir, out = []) {
36	  for (const e of readdirSync(dir)) {
37	    const p = join(dir, e);
38	    if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) walk(p, out);
39	    else out.push(p);
40	  }
41	  return out;
42	}
43	
44	// ---------- 主流程 ----------
45	mkdirSync(DST, { recursive: true });
46	const atlas = { files: {}, guidToFile: {} };
47	let totalSprites = 0;
48	
49	const pngs = walk(SRC).filter((p) => p.endsWith('.png'));
50	for (const png of pngs) {
51	  const metaPath = png + '.meta';
52	  if (!existsSync(metaPath)) continue;
53	  const { guid, sprites, idToName } = parseMeta(readFileSync(metaPath, 'utf8'));
54	  const rel = relative(SRC, png);
55	  const outPath = join(DST, rel);
56	  mkdirSync(join(outPath, '..'), { recursive: true });
57	  copyFileSync(png, outPath);
58	  atlas.files[rel] = { guid, sprites, idToName };
59	  if (guid) atlas.guidToFile[guid] = rel;
60	  totalSprites += sprites.length;
61	  if (sprites.length) console.log(`${rel}: ${sprites.length} sprites`);
62	}
63	
64	// ---------- Resources 数据表（物品/药水/配饰/瓦片 .asset YAML）----------
65	// YAML 双引号字符串里的 \uXXXX 转义需要解码（Unity 对非 ASCII 名字的存储方式）
66	function yamlUnescape(s) {
67	  return (s || '').replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g, (_, h) => String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)));
68	}
69	
70	function parseAsset(text) {
71	  const out = { fields: {} };
72	  out.name = yamlUnescape((text.match(/m_Name: "?([^"\n]+)"?/) || [])[1]);
73	  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
74	    const m = line.match(/^  (\w+): ?(.*)$/);
75	    if (m && !['m_Name', 'm_ObjectHideFlags', 'm_EditorClassIdentifier'].includes(m[1])) {
76	      out.fields[m[1]] = m[2].trim();
77	    }
78	  }
79	  return out;
80	}
81	
82	const data = { items: [], tiles: [], potions: [], accessories: [], buffs: [], anims: {} };
83	if (existsSync(RES)) {
84	  const assets = walk(RES).filter((p) => p.endsWith('.asset'));
85	  for (const a of assets) {
86	    const text = readFileSync(a, 'utf8');
87	    if (a.includes('/Items/Potions/')) {
88	      const d = parseAsset(text);
89	      const iconGuid = (text.match(/icon: \{fileID: \d+, guid: (\w+)/) || [])[1];
90	      data.potions.push({
91	        name: d.name, type: d.fields.type, iconGuid,
92	        buffType: d.fields.buffType ? +d.fields.buffType : null,
93	        duration: d.fields.duration ? +d.fields.duration : null,
94	        isHealType: d.fields.isHealType,
95	      });
96	    } else if (a.includes('/Items/Accessories/')) {
97	      const d = parseAsset(text);
98	      const iconGuid = (text.match(/icon: \{fileID: \d+, guid: (\w+)/) || [])[1];
99	      data.accessories.push({ name: d.name, type: d.fields.type, iconGuid });
100	    } else if (a.includes('/Items/')) {
101	      const d = parseAsset(text);
102	      const iconGuid = (text.match(/icon: \{fileID: \d+, guid: (\w+)/) || [])[1];
103	      const placeTile = (text.match(/placeTile: \{fileID: \d+, guid: (\w+)/) || [])[1];
104	      const funcList = (text.match(/funcList: ?(.*)/) || [])[1];
105	      data.items.push({
106	        name: d.name, type: d.fields.type, iconGuid, placeTile, funcList,
107	        file: relative(RES, a),
108	      });
109	    } else if (a.includes('/Tiles/')) {
110	      const d = parseAsset(text);
111	      const dropItem = (text.match(/dropItem: \{fileID: \d+, guid: (\w+)/) || [])[1];
112	      const tile = (text.match(/tile: \{fileID: \d+, guid: (\w+)/) || [])[1];
113	      data.tiles.push({
114	        name: d.name, tileGuid: tile, layer: d.fields.layer,
115	        isDestructible: d.fields.isDestructible, digList: d.fields.digList,
116	        digTime: d.fields.digTime, dropItemGuid: dropItem, dropCount: d.fields.dropCount,
117	        file: relative(RES, a),
118	      });
119	    }
120	  }
121	}
122	
123	// ---------- Buff 预制体图标（Prefabs/Buff/*.prefab → icon guid）----------
124	const BUFF_DIR = join(SRC, '..', 'Prefabs', 'Buff');
125	if (existsSync(BUFF_DIR)) {
126	  for (const f of readdirSync(BUFF_DIR).filter((x) => x.endsWith('.prefab'))) {
127	    const text = readFileSync(join(BUFF_DIR, f), 'utf8');
128	    const name = yamlUnescape((text.match(/m_Name: "?([^"\n]+)"?/) || [])[1]);
129	    const iconGuid = (text.match(/icon: \{fileID: \d+, guid: (\w+)/) || [])[1];
130	    data.buffs.push({ name, iconGuid });
131	  }
132	}
133	
134	// ---------- 动画帧序列（Animations/**/*.anim → 按时间排序的精灵帧）----------
135	const ANIM_DIR = join(SRC, '..', 'Animations');
136	if (existsSync(ANIM_DIR)) {
137	  for (const a of walk(ANIM_DIR).filter((p) => p.endsWith('.anim'))) {
138	    const text = readFileSync(a, 'utf8');
139	    // 关键帧: time + value{fileID,guid}
140	    const keys = [];
141	    const kRe = /- time: ([\d.e-]+)\n\s+value: \{fileID: (-?\d+), guid: (\w+), type: 3\}/g;
142	    let m;
143	    while ((m = kRe.exec(text))) keys.push({ t: +m[1], fileID: m[2], guid: m[3] });
144	    if (!keys.length) continue;
145	    keys.sort((p, q) => p.t - q.t);
146	    const frames = keys.map((k) => {
147	      const file = atlas.guidToFile[k.guid];
148	      const sprite = file ? atlas.files[file].idToName?.[k.fileID] : null;
149	      return { file, sprite };
150	    }).filter((f) => f.sprite);
151	    if (frames.length) {
152	      const relAnim = relative(ANIM_DIR, a).replace(/\.anim$/, '');
153	      data.anims[relAnim] = frames;
154	    }
155	  }
156	}
157	
158	writeFileSync(join(DST, 'atlas.json'), JSON.stringify(atlas));
159	
160	// ---------- RuleTile 规则（Assets/Tiles/Rules/*.asset）----------
161	// 邻居语义（按其脚本惯例）：3=同类 5=异类 6=同类(宽松)，NeighborPositions 为四邻偏移
162	const RULES_DIR = join(SRC, '..', 'Tiles', 'Rules');
163	const rules = {};
164	if (existsSync(RULES_DIR)) {
165	  for (const f of readdirSync(RULES_DIR).filter((x) => x.endsWith('.asset'))) {
166	    const text = readFileSync(join(RULES_DIR, f), 'utf8');
167	    const name = yamlUnescape((text.match(/m_Name: (.+)/) || [])[1]?.trim());
168	    const resolve = (ref) => {
169	      const gm = ref.match(/guid: (\w+)/);
170	      const fm = ref.match(/fileID: (-?\d+)/);
171	      if (!gm || !fm || fm[1] === '0') return null;
172	      const file = atlas.guidToFile[gm[1]];
173	      if (!file) return null;
174	      const spriteName = atlas.files[file].idToName?.[fm[1]];
175	      return spriteName ? { file, sprite: spriteName } : null;
176	    };
177	    const defaultSprite = resolve(text.match(/m_DefaultSprite: \{[^}]+\}/)?.[0] || '');
178	    const ruleBlocks = text.split('- m_Id: ').slice(1).map((b) => '- m_Id: ' + b);
179	    const tilingRules = [];
180	    for (const rb of ruleBlocks) {
181	      const id = +rb.match(/- m_Id: (\d+)/)[1];
182	      const spriteRefs = [...rb.matchAll(/\{fileID: -?\d+, guid: \w+, type: 3\}/g)].map((x) => resolve(x[0]));
183	      const neighbors = (rb.match(/m_Neighbors: ([0-9a-f]+)\n/) || [])[1] || '';
184	      // m_Neighbors 是每邻居 4 字节的小端 int32 数组（05 00 00 00 = 5），取每组的低字节
185	      const nbBytes = neighbors.match(/.{8}/g)?.map((h) => parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16)) ?? [];
186	      const positions = [...rb.matchAll(/- \{x: (-?\d+), y: (-?\d+), z: \d+\}/g)].map((p) => [ +p[1], +p[2] ]);
187	      const transform = +((rb.match(/m_RuleTransform: (\d+)/) || [])[1] ?? 0);
188	      const output = +((rb.match(/m_Output: (\d+)/) || [])[1] ?? 0);
189	      tilingRules.push({ id, sprites: spriteRefs.filter(Boolean), neighbors: nbBytes, positions, transform, output });
190	    }
191	    rules[name] = { defaultSprite, tilingRules };
192	  }
193	}
194	data.rules = rules;
195	
196	writeFileSync(join(DST, 'resources.json'), JSON.stringify(data, null, 1));
197	console.log(`\nDONE: ${pngs.length} PNGs, ${totalSprites} sprites, ${data.items.length} items, ${data.tiles.length} tiles, ${Object.keys(rules).length} ruleTiles → ${DST}`);
198
```

```
22	    playerSheet: generatePlayerSheet(),
23	    enemySprites: generateEnemySprites(),
24	    playerFrameW: PLAYER_FRAME_W,
25	    playerFrameH: PLAYER_FRAME_H,
26	  };
27	  return shared;
28	}
29	let shared: AssetBundle | null = null;
30	

```

```
7	function mk(w: number, h: number): [HTMLCanvasElement, CanvasRenderingContext2D] {
8	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
9	  c.width = w; c.height = h;
10	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
11	  return [c, ctx];
12	}
13	
14	function tileThumb(tileKey: string): HTMLCanvasElement {
15	  // 从 tile sheet 提取一个 16×16 缩样（用 mask=0 全边框帧）
16	  const [c, ctx] = mk(16, 16);
17	  // 直接画简化方块
18	  const m = matForTile(tileKey);
19	  const px = ctx.createImageData(16, 16);
20	  for (let y = 0; y < 16; y++) {
21	    for (let x = 0; x < 16; x++) {
22	      const n = hash2(x, y, TILE_BY_KEY[tileKey] ?? 3);
23	      let f = 0.92 + n * 0.16;
24	      if (y === 0 || x === 0) f *= 1.18;
25	      if (y === 15 || x === 15) f *= 0.82;
26	      const v = parseInt(shade(m.base, f).slice(1), 16);
27	      const i = (y * 16 + x) * 4;
28	      px.data[i] = (v >> 16) & 255; px.data[i + 1] = (v >> 8) & 255; px.data[i + 2] = v & 255; px.data[i + 3] = 255;
29	    }
30	  }
31	  ctx.putImageData(px, 0, 0);
32	  return c;
33	}
34	
35	function matForTile(tileKey: string) {
36	  switch (tileKey) {
37	    case 'dirt': return MATS.dirt;
38	    case 'stone': return MATS.stone;
39	    case 'sand': return MATS.sand;
40	    case 'snow': return MATS.snow;
41	    case 'wood': return MATS.wood;
42	    case 'platform': return MATS.wood;
43	    default: return MATS.wood;
44	  }
45	}
46	
47	function drawTool(kind: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer', metal: Mat): HTMLCanvasElement {
48	  const [c, ctx] = mk(16, 16);
49	  const px = ctx.createImageData(16, 16);
50	  const set = (x: number, y: number, col: string) => {
51	    if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x > 15 || y > 15) return;
52	    const v = parseInt(col.slice(1), 16);
53	    const i = (y * 16 + x) * 4;
54	    px.data[i] = (v >> 16) & 255; px.data[i + 1] = (v >> 8) & 255; px.data[i + 2] = v & 255; px.data[i + 3] = 255;
55	  };
56	  // 柄：左下到右上
57	  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
58	    const x = 3 + i, y = 13 - i;
59	    set(x, y, i % 3 === 0 ? MATS.woodDark.dark : MATS.wood.base);
60	    set(x + 1, y, MATS.woodDark.dark);
61	  }
62	  if (kind === 'pick') {
63	    // 镐头：弧形横跨右上
64	    const head = [[6, 2], [7, 1], [8, 1], [9, 1], [10, 1], [11, 2], [12, 3], [13, 4], [5, 3], [4, 4], [3, 5]];
65	    for (const [x, y] of head) {
66	      set(x, y, metal.light);
67	      set(x, y + 1, metal.base);
68	    }
69	    set(8, 2, metal.light2);
70	  } else if (kind === 'axe') {
71	    // 斧刃：右上三角
72	    for (let y = 1; y <= 6; y++) {
73	      for (let x = 9; x <= 14; x++) {
74	        if (x - 9 + (6 - y) < 5 && x + y > 12) set(x, y, (x === 9 || y === 6) ? metal.dark : metal.base);
75	      }
76	    }
77	    set(12, 1, metal.light); set(13, 2, metal.light);
78	  } else if (kind === 'hammer') {
79	    // 锤：方形锤头
80	    for (let y = 1; y <= 6; y++) {
81	      for (let x = 8; x <= 14; x++) {
82	        if (x === 8 || y === 1 || y === 6) set(x, y, metal.dark);
83	        else set(x, y, (x + y) % 3 === 0 ? metal.light : metal.base);
84	      }
85	    }
86	    set(11, 3, metal.light2);
87	  } else {
88	    // 剑：菱形刃
89	    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
90	      const wdt = i < 2 ? 1 : (i < 6 ? 2 : 1);
91	      const cx = 11, y = 1 + i;
92	      for (let dx = -wdt; dx <= wdt; dx++) {
93	        set(cx + dx, y, dx === 0 ? metal.light2 : (dx < 0 ? metal.light : metal.base));
94	      }
95	    }
96	    // 护手与柄
97	    for (let x = 7; x <= 15; x++) set(x, 10, x === 11 ? metal.light : metal.dark);
98	    set(10, 11, MATS.woodDark.base); set(10, 12, MATS.woodDark.dark);
99	  }
100	  ctx.putImageData(px, 0, 0);
101	  return c;
102	}
103	
104	function drawBar(metal: Mat): HTMLCanvasElement {
105	  const [c, ctx] = mk(16, 16);
106	  const px = ctx.createImageData(16, 16);
107	  const set = (x: number, y: number, col: string) => {

111	  };
112	  // 梯形锭

116	      set(x, y, y === 6 ? metal.light2 : y === 11 ? metal.dark : metal.base);
117	    }
118	  }

124	function drawOreIcon(metal: Mat): HTMLCanvasElement {
125	  const [c, ctx] = mk(16, 16);
126	  const px = ctx.createImageData(16, 16);

133	    }
134	  }
135	  const rng = new RNG(9);

138	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
139	      const i = ((by + dy) * 16 + bx + dx) * 4;
140	      const v = parseInt(metal.light.slice(1), 16);
141	      px.data[i] = (v >> 16) & 255; px.data[i + 1] = (v >> 8) & 255; px.data[i + 2] = v & 255;
142	    }
143	  }

145	  return c;

148	function simple(color: string, shape: (set: (x: number, y: number, c: string) => void) => void): HTMLCanvasElement {
149	  const [c, ctx] = mk(16, 16);

164	function genItemIcon(key: string): HTMLCanvasElement {
165	  const def = ITEM_DEFS[ITEM_BY_KEY[key]];
166	  const metals: Record<string, Mat> = {
167	    copper: MATS.copper, iron: MATS.iron, silver: MATS.silver, gold: MATS.gold,
168	  };
169	  // 方块类
170	  if (def.tile && ['dirt', 'stone', 'sand', 'snow', 'wood', 'platform'].includes(def.tile)) {
171	    return tileThumb(def.tile);
172	  }
173	  if (def.tile === 'torch') {
174	    return simple('#000', (s) => {
175	      for (let y = 7; y <= 14; y++) { s(7, y, MATS.wood.base); s(8, y, MATS.wood.dark); }
176	      s(7, 4, MATS.fire.light2); s(8, 4, MATS.fire.light2);
177	      s(6, 5, MATS.fire.light); s(7, 5, MATS.fire.base); s(8, 5, MATS.fire.base); s(9, 5, MATS.fire.light);
178	      s(7, 6, MATS.fire.dark); s(8, 6, MATS.fire.dark);
179	    });
180	  }
181	  if (def.tile === 'workbench') {
182	    return simple('#000', (s) => {
183	      for (let x = 1; x <= 14; x++) for (let y = 1; y <= 5; y++) s(x, y, y === 1 ? MATS.wood.light : MATS.wood.base);
184	      for (let y = 6; y <= 14; y++) { s(2, y, MATS.wood.dark); s(13, y, MATS.wood.dark); }
185	    });
186	  }
187	  if (def.tile === 'furnace') {
188	    return simple('#000', (s) => {
189	      for (let x = 1; x <= 14; x++) for (let y = 1; y <= 14; y++) s(x, y, (x === 1 || y === 1) ? MATS.stone.light : (x === 14 || y === 14) ? MATS.stone.dark : MATS.stone.base);
190	      for (let x = 5; x <= 10; x++) for (let y = 8; y <= 13; y++) s(x, y, y > 11 ? MATS.fire.light : MATS.fire.base);
191	    });
192	  }
193	  if (def.tile === 'anvil') {
194	    return simple('#000', (s) => {
195	      for (let x = 2; x <= 13; x++) { s(x, 3, MATS.metal.light2); s(x, 4, MATS.metal.base); }
196	      for (let y = 5; y <= 7; y++) for (let x = 6; x <= 9; x++) s(x, y, MATS.metal.dark);
197	      for (let x = 4; x <= 11; x++) { s(x, 8, MATS.metal.base); s(x, 9, MATS.metal.dark); }
198	    });
199	  }
200	  if (def.tile === 'chest') {
201	    return simple('#000', (s) => {
202	      for (let x = 2; x <= 13; x++) for (let y = 3; y <= 13; y++) s(x, y, y < 7 ? MATS.woodDark.light : MATS.woodDark.base);
203	      for (let x = 2; x <= 13; x++) s(x, 7, '#5A4520');
204	      s(7, 6, MATS.gold.light); s(8, 6, MATS.gold.light); s(7, 7, MATS.gold.base); s(8, 7, MATS.gold.base); s(7, 8, MATS.gold.dark); s(8, 8, MATS.gold.dark);
205	      for (let y = 3; y <= 13; y++) { s(2, y, MATS.gold.dark); s(13, y, MATS.gold.dark); }
206	    });
207	  }
208	  if (def.tile === 'door') {
209	    return simple('#000', (s) => {
210	      for (let x = 3; x <= 12; x++) for (let y = 1; y <= 14; y++) s(x, y, (x === 3 || x === 12 || y === 1 || y === 14) ? MATS.wood.dark : MATS.wood.base);
211	      s(7, 8, MATS.gold.light); s(8, 8, MATS.gold.light);
212	    });
213	  }
214	  // 工具（锤补入分派数组——drawTool 已有锤头分支，此前被漏过滤到此后的兜底图）
215	  if (def.tool && ['pick', 'axe', 'sword', 'hammer'].includes(def.tool.type)) {
216	    // vi_<id>_ 前缀先剥离再找金属段（vi_3509_copper_pickaxe → copper；
217	    // 此前 split('_')[0] 对 vi_* 键恒取 'vi' → 全部落到木色回退）
218	    const segs = key.replace(/^vi_\d+_/, '').split('_');
219	    const tier = segs.find((s) => metals[s]) ?? segs[0];
220	    const metal = metals[tier] ?? MATS.wood;
221	    return drawTool(def.tool.type as 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer', metal);
222	  }
223	  // 锭

226	  }
227	  // 矿石

229	    return drawOreIcon(metals[key.replace('_ore', '')] ?? MATS.copper);
230	  }
231	  // (本地键专属像素画分支 gel/mushroom_item/lens/suspicious_eye/
232	  //  lesser_healing_potion/coin_copper 已随本地物品退役移除——vi_ 物品图标
233	  //  走原版 Item_Atlas,此兜底仅服务残余 place_v_/无素材条目)
234	  // 兜底：问号块
235	  return simple('#888', (s) => {
236	    for (let x = 3; x <= 12; x++) for (let y = 3; y <= 12; y++) s(x, y, '#777');
237	  });
238	}

240	export function generateAllItemIcons(): Map<number, HTMLCanvasElement> {
241	  const map = new Map<number, HTMLCanvasElement>();

244	  }
245	  return map;
```

```
26	  tile: HTMLCanvasElement;   // atlas 页·tile 层（画在水之上）

31	  cell: number;
32	}
33	
34	// ---- 油漆乘色着色画布（ChunkCache 静态烘焙消费，world/Paint.applyPaintTint） ----
35	// 原版走 GPU shader（TilePaintSystemV2.cs:69-82）；Canvas 2D 用三段合成等价实现：
36	//   ① 摘出待着色区域 → ② multiply（负相 30 用 difference 反转）填色 →
37	//   ③ destination-in 按原区域 alpha 裁回（multiply 会把透明像素变成实色，必须裁）
38	// 全局单例：每 chunk 烘焙是串行的，16×16 复用零分配
39	const tintCanvas = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.createElement('canvas') : null;
40	if (tintCanvas) { tintCanvas.width = TILE; tintCanvas.height = TILE; }
41	const tintCtx = tintCanvas ? tintCanvas.getContext('2d') : null;
42	
43	/** 对 canvas 的 (readX,readY) 16×16 区域按 paint 着色,回写到 ctx 的 (writeX,writeY)。
44	 *  ★读/写坐标分离(2026-08-18 atlas 化 review 修):atlas 页路径读=页内绝对、写=cell 内
45	 *  局部(ctx 挂 translate(ox,oy));旧签名读写同参 → 回写落 (ox+px,oy+py) 双重偏移,
46	 *  ox>0 的 cell 油漆被 clip 挡掉 = 静默丢失(新世界无油漆的对拍探针抓不到) */
47	function tintRegion(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, src: HTMLCanvasElement, readX: number, readY: number, paint: number,
48	  writeX = readX, writeY = readY): void {
49	  if (!tintCtx || !tintCanvas) return;
50	  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
51	  tintCtx.clearRect(0, 0, TILE, TILE);
52	  tintCtx.drawImage(src, readX, readY, TILE, TILE, 0, 0, TILE, TILE);
53	  if (paint === 30) {
54	    // 负相漆：difference × 白 = 反转（MapHelper.MapColor :1849-1851 同式）
55	    tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'difference';
56	    tintCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
57	  } else {
58	    // 其余：multiply × paintColor = 逐通道乘色（白漆 26 乘白 ≡ 原色，自然等价）
59	    tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
60	    const [tr, tg, tb] = paintColor(paint);
61	    tintCtx.fillStyle = `rgb(${tr},${tg},${tb})`;
62	  }
63	  tintCtx.fillRect(0, 0, TILE, TILE);
64	  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
65	  tintCtx.drawImage(src, readX, readY, TILE, TILE, 0, 0, TILE, TILE);
66	  tintCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
67	  // 不 clearRect 直接覆盖回写：着色像素与本区域原内容 alpha 完全一致，
68	  // source-over 叠加即替换色值；clearRect 反而会打穿邻格溢出的跨格精灵
69	  ctx.drawImage(tintCanvas, writeX, writeY);
70	}

95	
96	  // ---- chunk atlas 页池(2026-08-18 IOSurface 张数优化) ----
97	  // 旧结构:每 chunk 2 张 256² canvas(稳态 35 chunk=70 张、满额 768 张),且
98	  // renderChunkInner 每次重烘焙【新建】画布——移动期 flushDirty 4 chunk/帧 =
99	  // 每帧 8 张新画布,GPU 进程 ~480 次/秒 IOSurface 分配/释放(双窗翻倍)。
100	  // 双开 GPU 爆的根因即此:macOS IOSurface 按【张】计费(mach port 级内核资源),
101	  // 字节无关(16×16 的分配也失败)——`--force-gpu-mem-available-mb` 只管 cc tile
102	  // 预算救不了(blink/common/switches.cc 注释实证)。
103	  // 页化:墙/tile 各一摞 1024² 页(4×4 cell/页),活张数 ≤ 2×ceil(N/16)
104	  // (稳态 ~6 张、满额 50 张),重烘焙 = clip+translate 原位重画 cell,
105	  // 运行期画布创建/销毁 = 0(页只在 dispose/退出世界时销毁)。
106	  private static readonly CELLS_PER_PAGE = 16;
107	  private static readonly PAGE_COLS = 4;
108	  private wallPages: HTMLCanvasElement[] = [];
109	  private tilePages: HTMLCanvasElement[] = [];
110	  /** 每页在用 cell 数(与 wallPages 同长;页全空可被 trimFreePages 回收——
111	   *  熔断软收缩路径的显存释放在 atlas 化后不能只还 cell 不放页,每页 2×4MB) */
112	  private pageUsed: number[] = [];
113	  /** 空闲 cell 栈(page*16+slot;栈顶复用 = 热页优先) */
114	  private cellFree: number[] = [];
115	  /** 调试/F5:当前 atlas 页数(墙+tile 双层各一摞,画布张数 = 2×页数) */
116	  get pageCount(): number { return this.wallPages.length; }
117	
118	  /** 归还 chunk cell(★页不销毁——重烘焙/换 chunk 复用同一批页,零画布churn)。
119	   *  所有丢弃旧 pair 的路径(标脏重建/LRU 淘汰/全量标脏)都必须先过这里;
120	   *  外部 stub(测试)/独立目标(cell=-1)无页可还 = no-op */
121	  releasePair(pair: ChunkPair | undefined): void {
122	    const c = pair?.cell;
123	    if (typeof c !== 'number' || c < 0) return;
124	    this.cellFree.push(c);
125	    this.pageUsed[Math.floor(c / ChunkCache.CELLS_PER_PAGE)]--;
126	  }
127	
128	  /** 回收尾部全空页(GPU 软收缩后调用:LRU 淘汰只还 cell,页的 4MB 背板仍在;
129	   *  中段空页留待复用不毁——索引稳定性,只从尾部收缩) */
130	  trimFreePages(): void {
131	    while (this.wallPages.length && this.pageUsed[this.wallPages.length - 1] === 0) {
132	      const wp = this.wallPages.pop()!, tp = this.tilePages.pop()!;
133	      this.pageUsed.pop();
134	      wp.width = 0; wp.height = 0;
135	      tp.width = 0; tp.height = 0;
136	    }
137	    const live = this.wallPages.length * ChunkCache.CELLS_PER_PAGE;
138	    if (this.cellFree.some((c) => c >= live)) {
139	      this.cellFree = this.cellFree.filter((c) => c < live);
140	    }
141	  }
142	
143	  private allocCell(): number {
144	    if (!this.cellFree.length) {
145	      const pageIdx = this.wallPages.length;
146	      const S = CHUNK * TILE * ChunkCache.PAGE_COLS;
147	      const mkPage = (): HTMLCanvasElement => {
148	        const c = document.createElement('canvas');
149	        if (ChunkCache.CPU_RENDER) { c.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true }); }
150	        c.width = S; c.height = S;
151	        return c;
152	      };
153	      this.wallPages.push(mkPage());
154	      this.tilePages.push(mkPage());
155	      this.pageUsed.push(0);
156	      for (let s = ChunkCache.CELLS_PER_PAGE - 1; s >= 0; s--) {
157	        this.cellFree.push(pageIdx * ChunkCache.CELLS_PER_PAGE + s);
158	      }
159	    }
160	    const cell = this.cellFree.pop()!;
161	    this.pageUsed[Math.floor(cell / ChunkCache.CELLS_PER_PAGE)]++;
162	    return cell;
163	  }
164	
165	  dispose(): void {
166	    // 页级释放(width=0 即刻归还 GPU 背板);cell/chunk 表随页一起清
167	    for (const p of this.wallPages) { p.width = 0; p.height = 0; }
168	    for (const p of this.tilePages) { p.width = 0; p.height = 0; }
169	    this.wallPages.length = 0;
170	    this.tilePages.length = 0;
171	    this.pageUsed.length = 0;
172	    this.cellFree.length = 0;
173	    this.chunks.clear();
174	    this.dirtyQueue.length = 0;
175	    this.dirtySet.clear();
176	    this.animChunksBySheet.clear();
177	    this.animChunksByWall.clear();
178	    if (this.bakeArriveTimer) { clearTimeout(this.bakeArriveTimer); this.bakeArriveTimer = 0; }
179	    this.chunkSheets.clear();
180	    this.arriveFiles.clear();

```

```
71	  frameCache.set(key, c);
```

```
5267	      originYOff?: number;        // origin.Y 自帧底上抬（576/577=4、696=4、564/565=10、668=4）
5268	      padX?: number; padY?: number; // Frame 第 5/6 参 padding（668 各 2）
5269	      inflateY?: number;          // Inflate(0,-n)：sy+=n/2、sh-=n（657 = 2）
5270	      mirrorTexFaces?: 'left' | 'right'; // 贴图默认朝向：left=朝右翻、right=朝左翻
5271	      rotate?: number;
5272	      glowTex?: string;           // GlowMask 同布局网格切片（564/565 = Glow_225）
5273	      echoRowShift?: number;      // 投影层：同列 row+n 再画一遍半透明（696 = +18）
5274	    } = {},
5275	  ) {
5276	    const ctx = this.ctx;
5277	    const perRow = opts.perRow ?? rows;
5278	    const col = Math.floor(idx / perRow), row = idx % perRow;
5279	    const padX = opts.padX ?? 0, padY = opts.padY ?? 0;
5280	    let sx = col * cellW + padX, sy = row * cellH + padY;
5281	    let sw = cellW - padX * 2, sh = cellH - padY * 2;
5282	    if (opts.inflateY) { sy += opts.inflateY >> 1; sh -= opts.inflateY; }
5283	    // 越界钳制（防御性：帧态漂移时不画到表外）
5284	    sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(img.width - 1, sx));
5285	    sy = Math.max(0, Math.min(img.height - 1, sy));
5286	    sw = Math.max(1, Math.min(sw, img.width - sx));
5287	    sh = Math.max(1, Math.min(sh, img.height - sy));

5293	    const originY = sh - (opts.originYOff ?? 0);

5295	    ctx.translate(e.cx, e.y + e.h + (opts.yOff ?? 0));
```

```
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID, ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';

/** 原版物品 id → 注册 key(查不到返回 '') */
export function VI(vid: number): string {
  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? '';
}

/** 原版物品 id → 内部 id(查不到返回 0) */
export function VI_ID(vid: number): number {
  const k = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
  return k !== undefined ? (ITEM_BY_KEY[k] ?? 0) : 0;
}

/** 高频消费点语义常量(grep 唯一入口;数值 = 原版 item id 权威) */
export const VI_KEY = {
  // 基础方块/材料
  DIRT: VI(2), STONE: VI(3), WOOD: VI(9), SAND: VI(169), SNOW: VI(593),
  CLAY: VI(133), GLASS: VI(170), MUD: VI(176), OBSIDIAN: VI(173), SILT: VI(424),
  ICE: VI(664), GRAY_BRICK: VI(129), RED_BRICK: VI(131),
  GEL: VI(23), ACORN: VI(27), MUSHROOM: VI(5), LENS: VI(38), ROPE: VI(965),
  BOMB: VI(166), WIRE: VI(530),
  // 放置/功能物
  TORCH: VI(8), PLATFORM: VI(94), DOOR: VI(25), WORKBENCH: VI(36), FURNACE: VI(33),
  ANVIL: VI(35), CHEST: VI(48), SIGN: VI(171),
  TABLE: VI(32), CHAIR: VI(34), BED: VI(224), TOMBSTONE: VI(321), LIFE_CRYSTAL: VI(29),
  // 桶族/水蜡
  EMPTY_BUCKET: VI(205), WATER_BUCKET: VI(206), LAVA_BUCKET: VI(207), HONEY_BUCKET: VI(1128),
  WATER_CANDLE: VI(148),
  // 药水(常用)
  LESSER_HEAL: VI(28), HEAL: VI(188),
  // Boss/入侵召唤
  SUSPICIOUS_EYE: VI(43), WORM_FOOD: VI(70), BLOODY_SPINE: VI(1331), SLIME_CROWN: VI(560),
  GOBLIN_STANDARD: VI(361), SNOW_GLOBE: VI(602), PIRATE_MAP: VI(1315),
  MECH_WORM: VI(556), MECH_EYE: VI(544), MECH_SKULL: VI(557),
  // 开局三件套(原版新玩家:Copper Pickaxe/Shortsword/Axe)
  STARTER: { PICK: VI(3509), SHORTSWORD: VI(3507), AXE: VI(3506) },
  // 钱币(单轨,Inventory.COIN_KEYS 同源)
  COIN: { COPPER: VI(71), SILVER: VI(72), GOLD: VI(73), PLATINUM: VI(74) },
  // 饰品(退役本地 accessory 枚举的继任)
  LUCKY_HORSESHOE: VI(158), FERAL_CLAWS: VI(211),
  // 预览盔甲(铁三件)
  IRON_HELMET: VI(90), IRON_CHAINMAIL: VI(81), IRON_GREAVES: VI(77),
  // 草种(蘑菇 194 等)
  MUSHROOM_GRASS_SEEDS: VI(194),
  // 特殊工具:传送门枪 3384(Item.cs:30926-30942;月总宝藏袋 3332 授予 :7363-7366)
  PORTAL_GUN: VI(3384),
} as const;
```

```
191	        url = c.toDataURL();
192	        iconCache.set(id, url);
```

```
// 用法：node scripts/asset-audit.mjs
// 输出需要重新解包的 Tiles_N.png 列表（TEdit 需要的帧超出 PNG 范围 = 素材版本旧于数据）
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ASSETS = join(here, '..', '..', 'terraria-assets', 'Images');
const TEDIT_DATA = join(here, '..', '..', 'Terraria-Map-Editor', 'src', 'TEdit.Terraria', 'Data');

function pngSize(file) {
  const b = readFileSync(file);
  return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };
}

const tiles = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'tiles.json'), 'utf8'));
const whitelist = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, 'vanilla-whitelist.json'), 'utf8'));

const bad = [];
for (const id of whitelist.tiles) {
  const t = tiles.find((e) => e.id === id);
  if (!t) continue;
  const file = join(ASSETS, `Tiles_${id}.png`);
  let size;
  try { size = pngSize(file); } catch { bad.push({ id, name: t.name, reason: 'PNG 缺失' }); continue; }
  const grid = t.textureGrid ?? [16, 16];
  const gap = t.frameGap ?? [2, 2];
  const stride = [grid[0] + gap[0], grid[1] + gap[1]];
  // 帧需求：TEdit frames uv 最大值 + 帧尺寸。
  // textureWrap（换行纹理）：超出 wrapThreshold 的帧换算回带内坐标，
  // 实际需求 = 带内最大值 + 一带宽度/高度（渲染端已实现带换行兼容，非素材缺失）
  const wrap = t.textureWrap ?? null;
  const wrapMax = wrap ? wrap.wrapThreshold : Infinity;
  const inc = wrap ? wrap.offsetIncrement : 0;
  const isU = wrap?.axis === 'U';
  let needW = 0, needH = 0, wrappedStyles = 0;
  for (const f of t.frames ?? []) {
    if (!f?.uv) continue;
    let ux = f.uv[0], uy = f.uv[1];
    if (wrap) {
      if (isU) { if (ux >= wrapMax) { ux = inc + (ux - wrapMax) % (Math.max(1, size.w - inc)); wrappedStyles++; } }
      else { if (uy >= wrapMax) { uy = inc + (uy - wrapMax) % (Math.max(1, size.h - inc)); wrappedStyles++; } }
    }
    needW = Math.max(needW, ux + grid[0]);
    needH = Math.max(needH, uy + grid[1]);
  }
  if (wrappedStyles) t._wrapped = wrappedStyles;
  if (needW > size.w || needH > size.h) {
    bad.push({
      id, name: t.name,
      png: `${size.w}×${size.h}`, need: `${needW}×${needH}`,
      short: needW > size.w ? `宽缺 ${needW - size.w}px` : `高缺 ${needH - size.h}px`,
      reason: t._wrapped ? `（${t._wrapped} 个换行样式）` : undefined,
    });
  }
}
if (bad.length) {
  console.log(`素材不达标（TEdit 帧需求超出 PNG 尺寸）共 ${bad.length} 张，需从当前版本游戏重新解包：`);
  for (const b of bad) console.log(`  Tiles_${b.id}.png [${b.name}] PNG ${b.png} → 需要 ${b.need}（${b.short}）${b.reason ? '· ' + b.reason : ''}`);
} else {
  console.log('全部达标');
}
const wrappedCount = tiles.filter((t) => t._wrapped).length;
if (wrappedCount) console.log(`（${wrappedCount} 张为换行纹理 textureWrap，渲染端带换行兼容，非素材缺失）`);
```

```
4	function mk(w: number, h: number): [HTMLCanvasElement, CanvasRenderingContext2D] {
5	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
6	  c.width = w; c.height = h;
7	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
8	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
9	  return [c, ctx];
10	}

12	class Px {
13	  img: ImageData;
14	  constructor(public ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, public w: number, public h: number, public ox = 0, public oy = 0) {
15	    this.img = ctx.createImageData(w, h);
16	  }
17	  set(x: number, y: number, color: string, a = 255) {
18	    if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= this.w || y >= this.h) return;
19	    const v = parseInt(color.slice(1), 16);
20	    const i = (y * this.w + x) * 4;
21	    this.img.data[i] = (v >> 16) & 255;
22	    this.img.data[i + 1] = (v >> 8) & 255;
23	    this.img.data[i + 2] = v & 255;
24	    this.img.data[i + 3] = a;
25	  }
26	  rect(x0: number, y0: number, w: number, h: number, color: string, a = 255) {
27	    for (let y = y0; y < y0 + h; y++) for (let x = x0; x < x0 + w; x++) this.set(x, y, color, a);
28	  }
29	  flush() { this.ctx.putImageData(this.img, this.ox, this.oy); }
30	}
31	
32	// 玩家：20 宽 × 42 高 hitbox，sprite 画 24×44，帧横排
33	// 形象参考原版轮廓比例（大头小身、刺猬棕发、蓝上衣棕长裤），像素画为原创绘制
34	// 布局（y 坐标全部落帧内）：发 0-8 / 头 6-17 / 躯干 19-30 / 腿 31-40 / 鞋 41-42
35	export const PLAYER_FRAME_W = 24;
36	export const PLAYER_FRAME_H = 44;

40	function drawPlayerFrame(px: Px, pose: Pose) {
41	  const skin = MATS.skin.base, skinDark = shade(MATS.skin.base, 0.82);
42	  const shirt = '#3E5CBE', shirtDark = '#2C4494', shirtLight = '#5A78D4';
43	  const pants = '#4A3826', pantsDark = '#382A1C';
44	  const hair = '#8A5A28', hairDark = '#6A4420';
45	  const boot = '#33241A';
46	  const eyeW = '#F0F0F0';
47	  const cx = 12;
48	  const bob = pose.bodyBob; // ±1，只作用于头/躯干/手臂，脚固定在地面
49	
50	  // ---- 头发（帧顶，y 0..8）----
51	  const hy = 0 + bob;
52	  px.rect(cx - 6, hy + 2, 12, 6, hair);            // 发主体
53	  px.rect(cx - 7, hy + 4, 2, 6, hair);             // 后脑勺
54	  px.rect(cx + 6, hy + 4, 2, 4, hair);             // 鬓角
55	  // 发刺（原版后掠感）
56	  px.set(cx - 5, hy + 0, hair); px.set(cx - 2, hy + 0, hair);
57	  px.set(cx + 2, hy + 0, hair); px.set(cx + 5, hy + 0, hair);
58	  px.set(cx - 4, hy + 1, hair); px.set(cx + 1, hy + 1, hair); px.set(cx + 4, hy + 1, hair);
59	  px.rect(cx - 6, hy + 7, 12, 1, hairDark);        // 刘海底边
```

```
178	 *  'legs'=腿皮肤/裤/鞋+腿甲——对应原版 headPosition/bodyPosition/legPosition 三层组
179	 *  （PlayerDrawLayers 逐层 + drawPlayer.headPosition/bodyPosition/legPosition 偏移） */
180	export type DollPart = 'head' | 'body' | 'legs';
181	/** body/legs 双行（原版身体帧 bodyFrame 与腿帧 legFrame 是两条独立状态机：
182	 *  Player.cs 腿链 :35718-35855、身体链 :35874-36186——use 行/静持行只钉身体，
183	 *  腿照常走 legFrame 循环；行走时身体同步腿（:36184-36186 bodyFrame.Y=legFrame.Y））。
184	 *  rows 模式烘焙出 40×56 单帧（身体件取 body 行、腿件取 legs 行） */
185	export interface DollRows { body: number; legs: number }
186	/** 坐姿腿切片参数（DrawSittingLegs PlayerDrawLayers.cs:1585-1735 照抄） */
187	export interface DollSitting {
188	  /** 腿甲槽位 id（特殊切片表按 legIndex 分档；0/-1=默认切片） */
189	  legIndex: number;
190	  /** seatYOffset = -4 + (int)offsetForSeat.Y（PlayerSittingHelper.cs:29-31）——
191	   *  149/169/238/223 族切片表内用 vector.Y -= seatYOffset 对冲，需逐座位传值 */
192	  seatYOffset: number;
193	  /** 身甲槽位 id（193/194 族只在 body==218 时改切片） */
194	  body: number;
195	}

197	 *  （DrawPlayer_21_Head_TheFace :2577 flag 门——狼人头 38 全脸、幽灵脸 135 等） */
198	export const HIDES_HEAD = new Set([38, 135, 269, 282, 288]);

200	/** DrawSittingLegs 切片计划（局部坐标 = 帧锚点左上、朝右空间；外层 translate 已含
201	 *  seatYOffset 的整体座位偏移，故 evY 中 -sY 的对冲项即恢复原版净偏移）。
202	 *  n 从 num3 降到 0：源条 = 帧 srcRow 起 y+2n+num2、高 (n==num3 ? 56-num2-2n : 2)；
203	 *  目标 = (xShift+num+num4·n+num6+(n?num7:0)+num8, evY+2+num2+num5+num9) */

208	  let num6 = 0, num7 = 0, num8 = 0, num9 = 0;
209	  let evY = 0, xShift = 0, row = srcRow, full = false;
210	  if (legIndex === 169 || !skin) {
211	    switch (legIndex) {
212	      case 217: num = 0; num4 = 0; num5 = 0; num3 = 1; num2 = 0; full = true; break;
213	      case 149: case 171: case 172:
214	        num = -6; num4 = 2; num5 = 2; num3 = 4; num2 = 6; evY = 6 - sY; break;
215	      case 169:
216	        if (skin) { num = -6; num4 = 2; num5 = 2; num3 = 4; num2 = 6; evY = 6 - sY; }
217	        else { num = 0; num4 = 0; num5 = 0; num3 = 1; num2 = 0; evY = -sY; }  // flag(长袍) 族忽略
218	        break;
219	      case 238: case 239: num = 2; num4 = 2; num5 = -2; num2 = 42; evY = -sY; break;
220	      case 214: case 215: case 216:
221	        num = -6; num4 = 2; num5 = 2; num3 = 4; num2 = 6; evY = 6; break;
222	      case 106: case 143: case 226:
223	        num = 0; num4 = 0; num2 = 6; num5 = 0; evY = 4; row = 5; break;
224	      case 222: xShift = -2; break;
225	      case 223: xShift = -2; evY = -sY; break;
226	      case 132: num = -2; num7 = 2; break;
227	      case 193: case 194: if (bodyIdx === 218) { num = -2; num7 = 2; evY = 2; } break;
228	      case 177: case 178: case 181: case 182: case 206:
```

```
58	export function dollHairLayer(a: Appearance, headIdx: number): DollHairLayer | null {
59	  const mode = headIdx === 0 || FULL_HAIR_HEADS.has(headIdx) ? 'full' as const
60	    : HAT_HAIR_HEADS.has(headIdx) ? 'alt' as const : null;
61	  if (!mode) return null;

71	export function hairFrameTinted(layer: DollHairLayer, row: number, color: { r: number; g: number; b: number }): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
72	  const rect = UITextures.get(layer.src);
73	  if (!rect) return null;

75	  const img = rect.img as HTMLImageElement;
76	  const sc = hairScratch ??= document.createElement('canvas');
77	  if (sc.width !== FRAME_W || sc.height !== FRAME_H) { sc.width = FRAME_W; sc.height = FRAME_H; }
78	  const sctx = sc.getContext('2d')!;
79	  sctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
80	  sctx.clearRect(0, 0, FRAME_W, FRAME_H);
81	  sctx.drawImage(img, 0, hr * FRAME_H, FRAME_W, FRAME_H, 0, 0, FRAME_W, FRAME_H);
82	  sctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
83	  sctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${color.r},${color.g},${color.b})`;
84	  sctx.fillRect(0, 0, FRAME_W, FRAME_H);
85	  sctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
86	  sctx.drawImage(img, 0, hr * FRAME_H, FRAME_W, FRAME_H, 0, 0, FRAME_W, FRAME_H);
87	  sctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
88	  return sc;
89	}
90
```

```
253	export function compositePaperDoll(
254	  a: Appearance,
255	  equip?: { head?: number | null; body?: number | null; legs?: number | null },
256	  opts?: { skipHair?: boolean; layer?: DollLayer; part?: DollPart; rows?: DollRows; sitting?: DollSitting },
257	): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
258	  const key = appearanceKey(a) + (equip ? `|e${equip.head ?? 0},${equip.body ?? 0},${equip.legs ?? 0}` : '')
259	    + (opts?.skipHair ? '|nohair' : '') + (opts?.layer ? `|L${opts.layer}` : '')
260	    + (opts?.part ? `|P${opts.part}` : '')
261	    + (opts?.rows ? `|R${opts.rows.body},${opts.rows.legs}` : '')
262	    + (opts?.sitting ? `|S${opts.sitting.legIndex},${opts.sitting.seatYOffset},${opts.sitting.body}` : '');
263	  const hit = cache.get(key);
264	  if (hit) return hit;
265	
266	  // 发型模式（GetHairSettings）：无头盔=完整；fullHair 头盔=完整；hatHair=帽子发型；其余=隐藏
267	  const headIdx = equip?.head ?? 0;
268	  const hairLayer = opts?.skipHair ? null : dollHairLayer(a, headIdx);
269	  const hairMode: 'full' | 'alt' | 'none' = !hairLayer ? 'none' : hairLayer.mode;
270	  const hairSrc = hairLayer ? hairLayer.src : '';
271	
272	  // ---- 懒加载就绪预检(vanilla-ui 按需加载后必需):任一必需贴图未就绪 → 返回 null
273	  // 且【不缓存】(查询本身触发后台加载,消费方下帧重试)。否则首帧的空纸娃娃会被
274	  // 永久缓存——角色选择界面人物贴图就再也出不来了
275	  // part 拆分时只预检该件所需通道（死亡散飞只走 head/body/legs 各自的层组）
276	  const part = opts?.part;
277	  const needSheets = part === 'head' ? new Set<number>([0, 1, 2])
278	    : part === 'body' ? new Set<number>([3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 13])
279	    : part === 'legs' ? new Set<number>([10, 11, 12])
280	    : new Set<number>([...VERTICAL_CHANNELS.map((ch) => ch.sheet), 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 13]);
281	  for (const s of needSheets) {
282	    if (!sheetRect(a.skinVariant, s)) return null;
283	  }
284	  if (hairMode !== 'none' && (!part || part === 'head') && !UITextures.get(hairSrc)) return null;
285	  if (equip?.legs && (!part || part === 'legs') && !UITextures.get(`Armor_Legs_${equip.legs}.png`)) return null;
286	  if (equip?.body && (!part || part === 'body') && !UITextures.get(`Armor_Armor_${equip.body}.png`)) return null;
287	  if (equip?.head && (!part || part === 'head') && !UITextures.get(`Armor_Head_${equip.head}.png`)) return null;
288	
289	  // rows 模式：单帧 40×56（body 件取 rows.body、legs 件取 rows.legs）
290	  const rowsMode = opts?.rows ?? null;
291	  const sit = opts?.sitting ?? null;
292	  const nFrames = rowsMode ? 1 : BODY_FRAMES;
293	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
294	  c.width = FRAME_W;
295	  // 坐姿切片族（177/106 等）dest 可伸到 y=64（超帧底 8px——原版 DrawData 无裁剪、
296	  // 站姿帧底本有 ~2px 透明边距，脚底锚由消费方 spriteBottomPad 自适应）——扩高防截
297	  c.height = nFrames * FRAME_H + (sit ? 8 : 0);
298	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
299	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
300	
301	  const hasBackHair = hairMode !== 'none' && backHairDraw(a.hair);
302	  const hairRect = hairMode !== 'none' ? UITextures.get(hairSrc) : null;
303	  const hairTinted = hairRect ? tint(hairRect.img as HTMLImageElement, a.hairColor) : null;
304	  const onlyArmor = opts?.layer === 'armor' || opts?.layer === 'armorNoHead';
305	  const noArmor = opts?.layer === 'skin';

322	  // ---- 竖条通道（腿/裤/鞋/头/眼：20 帧直排） ----

332	    const tinted = tint(img, color);
333	    const isLegCh = ch.sheet >= 10 && ch.sheet <= 12;
334	    for (let i = 0; i < nFrames; i++) {

336	      if (sit && isLegCh) {
```
